[\[3a.6\]]dgar [#]bcas nyan par mos shing yid dad 'dir shog shig_/bder gshegs chos kyi sku mnga' sras bcas dang /_/phyag 'os kun la'ang gus par phyag 'tshal te/_/bde [#]gshegs sras kyi sdom la 'jug pa rnams/_/mdor bsdus don gyi ngag gis brjod par bya/_/sngon

[\[3b.1\]]'gyur la/_/bdag dang skal ba mnyam pa gzhan gyis kyang /_/ji ste 'di dag mthong na don yod 'gyur/_/dal 'byor shin tu rnyed par dka' ba dang /_/skyes bu'i don bsgrub 'di ni rnyed gyur pas/_/gal te 'di la phan pa ma bsams na/_/phyis 'di [#]yang dag 'byor par ga la

[\[5b.2\]]lta bu'o/_/'khor ba'i chu'i nang na gnas pa steng du 'byin pas mchil pa lta bu'o/_/sgrib pa dang khebs pa'i rtsa ba [#]thams cad rnam par 'thor bas rlung gi dkyil 'khor lta bu'o/_/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dang smon lam thams cad bsdus pa'i phyir mdor bstan pa lta bu'o/_/
[\[5b.3\]]lha dang mi dang lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten la mchod rten lta bu ste/_rigs kyi bu de ltar byang chub kyi sems ni yon tan [#]de dag dang /_yon tan gyi bye brag tshad med pa gzhan dang yang ldan no zhes brjod do/_/so so'i skye bo la yang byang chub kyi sems skye ste/_'di tshig tsam ni [#]ma 
[\[5b.4\]]yin no zhes bya bar [#]ji ltar shes she na/_mdo sde du ma mthong ba las te/_re zhig 'phags pa dri ma med par grags pas bstan pa las/_'jig tshogs la lta ba ri rab dang mnyam pa dran [#]nas byang chub kyi sems skye zhing de las [#]sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams rnam par skye'o zhes ji skad du

[\[6a.1\]]du smra ba la khong khro ba byas pa yin no zhes gsungs so/_/nges pa dang ma nges pa la 'jug pa'i phyag rgya'i mdo las kyang /_/phyugs kyi shing rta lta bus 'gro ba'i byang chub sems dpa' gang zhe na/_'di lta ste dper na skyes bu la la zhig sangs rgyas kyi 
[\[6a.2\]]zhing lnga'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams 'da' bar 'dod nas/_des phyugs kyi shing rta la zhon te lam du zhugs nas/_de yun ring pos lam ring por dpag tshad brgyar phyin pa las/_de der rlung gi dkyil 'khor chen pos bzlog nas dpag tshad [#]khrir ded de 
[\[6a.3\]]phyir phul na skyes bu des phyugs kyi shing rtas 'jig rten gyi khams de dag 'da' ba'am bskal pa las brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i bskal par yang 'jig rten gyi khams gcig kyang 'da' bar nus sam gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das de ni ma lags so/_/
[\[6a.4\]]bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_de bzhin du gang la la zhig gis byang chub tu sems bskyed la theg pa chen po mi 'dzin mi klog par/_nyan thos kyi theg pa pa rnams la brten [#]cing de dag dang lhan cig tu sten par byed nyan thos kyi theg pa klog cing kha ton byed dpyod 
[\[6a.5\]]cing khong du chud par byed la/_gzhan dag kyang klog tu 'jug pa nas/_khong du chud par byed pa'i bar du byed pa ni[#]/_de ni des shes rab rtul por 'gyur te/_bla na med pa'i ye shes kyi lam nas slar drangs nas phyir ldog [#]go/_/byang chub sems dpa' 'di'i byang chub kyi sems 
[\[6a.6\]]bsgoms [#]pa las byung ba'i shes rab kyi dbang po dang /_shes rab kyi mig gang yin pa de yang rtul por 'gyur [#]zhing nyams par byed de 'di ni phyugs kyi shing rta lta bus 'gro ba'i byang chub sems dpa'o zhes bshad do/_/de bas na de ltar [#]de dag gi stong pa nyid la mos pa dang

[\[6b\]]
[\[6b.1\]]par brjod cing /_ting nge 'dzin dang gzungs dang rnam par thar pa dang mngon par shes pa dang rig pa grangs med pas rnam par rol pa/_chos mtha' yas pa la dga' zhing mos pas zang zing med pa phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal pa'i mur thug par rnam par mi rtog cing lhun gyis grub pa'i 
[\[6b.2\]]dga' ba'i shugs kyis [#]snang ba thob pa bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du ma dpag tu med par theg pa chen po la zhugs pa'i bsgom pa sna tshogs yongs su rdzogs pa gzhan gyi don gyi nan tan tshar phyin pa/_bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs mngon par bsgrub pa 
[\[6b.3\]]grub pa'i sngon gyi sbyor ba brgya stong phun sum tshogs par brjod do/_/ci ste 'di drang ba'i don yin na/_ci'i phyir byang chub tu sems bskyed pa gzhan sa 'di la mi 'dod/_'dir 'dod pa tsam gyis drang ba'i don du byed pa'i bye brag gi rtags kyang mi rnyed do/_/mos pas spyod pa 
[\[6b.4\]]chen po'i chos nyid du gsungs pa las kyang /_mos pas spyod pa 'bring dang chung ngu'i rnam pa yang ci nas yod pa nyid du rtogs so/_/yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba'i mdo las/_ji skad du bcom ldan 'das byang chub tu sems bskyed pa su'i lags/_rgyal po chen po 
[\[6b.5\]]gang gis lhag pa'i bsam pa ma 'khrugs pa'o/_/gsol pa bcom ldan 'das su'i lhag pa'i bsam pa ma 'khrugs [#]bka' stsal pa/_rgyal po chen po gang gi snying rje chen po skye ba'o/_/gsol pa bcom ldan 'das su'i snying rje chen po skye/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' 
[\[6b.6\]]stsal pa/_rgyal po [#]chen po gang gi [#]sems can thams cad la yongs su mi gtong ba'i sems skye ba'o/_/gsol pa bcom ldan 'das ji ltar sems can thams cad yongs su ma btang ba lags/_bka' stsal pa rgyal po chen po nam bdag bde ba btang ba yin no 
[\[6b.7\]]zhes gsungs pa'i dgongs pa ci zhig yin zhe na/_byang chub kyi sems tsam gyis chog par 'dzin pa rnams snying rje la dga' ba skyed [#]pa'i phyir 'di gsungs te/_dper na gang la rnal 'byor med pa de dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i bstan pa la rab tu byung [#]ba ma yin no zhes 
[\[7a\]]
[\[7a.1\]]gsungs pa lta bu'o/_/de bzhin du 'dir yang byang chub kyi sems gzhan smad par rig par bya'i/_byang chub kyi sems rnam pa gzhan du mi skye ba ni ma yin no/_/ji skad du chos bcu pa'i mdo las/_rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' rigs la gnas 
[\[7a.2\]]pa byang chub tu sems ma bskyed na/_de bzhin gshegs pa'am de bzhin gshegs pa'i nyan thos kyis [#]bskul zhing skyo bar byas/_yang dag par 'dzin du bcug ste bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed pa 'di ni byang chub kyi sems la mos pa'i rgyu dang po'o/_/
[\[7a.3\]]rdzogs pa'i byang chub bam byang chub kyi sems kyi bsngags pa brjod pa thos nas/_bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed pa 'di ni rgyu gnyis pa'o/_/sems can mgon med pa dang skyabs med pa dang dpung gnyen med pa rnams mthong nas snying rje'i sems 
[\[7a.4\]]nye bar gzhag [#]ste bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed pa 'di ni rgyu gsum pa'o/_/des de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog yongs su rdzogs pa mthong nas dga' ba bskyed de bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems 
[\[7a.5\]]bskyed pa 'di ni rgyu bzhi pa'o zhes gsungs pa bzhin no/_/byang chub kyi sems de ni rnam pa gnyis te/_byang chub tu smon pa'i sems dang byang chub tu chas pa'i sems so/_/ji skad du sdong po bkod pa'i mdo las/_rigs kyi bu sems can gang dag bla na med pa yang dag par 
[\[7a.6\]]rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu smon pa de dag ni sems can gyi khams na dkon no/_/de bas kyang gang dag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu chas pa'i sems can de dag ni shin tu dkon no zhes gsungs pa lta bu'o/_/de la byang chub tu smon pa'i sems ni ngas 
[\[7a.7\]]sangs rgyas su 'gyur bar bya'o zhes sems smon pa las skyes pa yin te/_'phags pa dpa' bar 'gro ba'i mdo las/_g.yo byas te byang chub kyi sems bskyed pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi rgyur gsungs na dge ba sna 'ga' byas te sems bskyed pa lta ci smos zhes gsungs 
[\[7b\]]
[\[7b.1\]]so/_/ji skad du bskal pa bzang po'i mdo las/_de bzhin gshegs pa dbyangs byin zhes bya ba la de bzhin gshegs pa skar ma'i rgyal po zhes bya ba ba lang rdzir gyur pas ba gu [#]la'i me tog phul nas dang po byang chub tu sems bskyed do/_/de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa klog [#]gi sgron 
[\[7b.2\]]ma zhes bya ba la de bzhin gshegs pa grags pa thag par gyur pas ras kyi kha tshar phul te/_dang po byang chub tu sems bskyed do/_/de bzhin du [#]de bzhin gshegs pa 'od mtha' yas zhes bya ba la de bzhin gshegs pa 'od 'phro can grong khyer gyi sprang por gyur pas rtsa'i sgron ma phul 
[\[7b.3\]]te [#]dang po byang chub tu sems bskyed do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par gnon brten [#]zhes bya ba la de bzhin gshegs pa thub dka' shing thun du gyur pas so shing phul te dang po byang chub tu sems bskyed do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa 'jam dpal grags zhes bya ba 
[\[7b.4\]]la de bzhin gshegs pa yon tan rgyal mtshan sman pa'i rgyal por gyur pas skyu ru ra cig phul nas dang po byang chub tu sems bskyed do zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs pa bzhin no/_/des kyang 'khor ba'i bde ba mtha' yas pa bskyed pa'i phyir byang chub kyi sems spyod pa

[\[17a\]]
[\[17a.1\]]'jig rten thams cad na bdag nyid ci 'dod pa bzhin du bya ba la dbang du gyur par mthong bas 'jig rten kun tu bdag nyid mchod rten gyi mchog tu 'gyur bar bya ste bdag nyid 'gro ba thams cad kyi mchod rten du mthong ba dang /_'jig rten kun du sems mnyam 
[\[17a.2\]]par bsgrub pa dang /_bdag nyid 'gro ba kun du yo byad thams cad kyi mu stegs su mthong ba dang /_bdag nyid 'jig rten thams cad la bde ba sbyin par so sor rtogs [#]pa dang /_bdag nyid 'gro ba kun gyi sbyin bdag tu mos pa dang /_bdag nyid 'jig rten kun gyi [#]shes pa dang 
[\[17a.3\]]'dra bar byas pa dang /_bdag nyid byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dang ldan par bskyed [#]pa dang /_bdag nyid ji skad smras pa de bzhin du byed pa la 'jug pa dang /_bdag nyid thams cad mkhyen pa'i go cha bgos par so sor rtogs [#]pa dang sngon mgron du gnyer ba'i sems can rjes su 
[\[17a.4\]]skyong ba dang /_bdag nyid nan tan la gnas par byed pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i gtong ba'i sems yid la byed cing sems can thams cad la bdag nyid skyed mos tshal dang 'dra bar lta ba dang /_'jig rten thams cad la bdag nyid chos kyi dga' ba dang 'dra bar ston 
[\[17a.5\]]pa dang /_bdag nyid sems can thams cad la yid bde ba sbyin pa nyid du gnas pa dang /_bdag nyid dga' ba mtha' yas pa bskyed par 'gro ba thams cad la gtong zhing 'jig rten kun du bdag nyid dkar po'i chos skye ba'i sgo lta bur yongs su 'dzin pa dang /_bdag nyid 
[\[17a.6\]]sems can thams cad la sangs rgyas kyi byang chub sbyin par smon lam 'debs pa dang /_skye dgu thams cad la bdag nyid pha dang 'dra bar 'jug pa dang /_sems can gyi khams thams cad du yo byad thams cad ma tshang ba med par bya ba'i phyir bdag nyid rab tu gnas pas [#]yin no/_/de 
[\[17a.7\]]ltar byang chub sems dpa' bdag nyid g.yog tu sbyin par byed pa na/_slong ba dag la dga' ba yid la byed pa'i sems dang ldan pa[#]/_sa la stan 'ding ba'i stan lta bu'i [#]sems dang ldan par bya ste/_sa dang 'dra bar sdug bsngal thams cad bzod pa yid la byed pa bsgrub cing /_sems 
[\[17b\]]
[\[17b.1\]]can thams cad kyi rim gro bya ba la ngal ba med pa'i yid dang ldan pa/_byis pa'i skye bos sbyar ba'i nyes byas dang du blang ba la brtan zhing sran thub pa'i rang bzhin can legs par gnas pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba la mngon par brtson zhing sems can gyi khams thams cad kyi [#]rim gro bya ba la rab tu 
[\[17b.2\]]zhugs pas [#]yin no/_/slong ba 'ongs pa dag la rna ba'am sna yongs su gtong ba na/_byang chub sems dpa'i [#]spyod pas zin cing de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyis [#]rigs mtho ba las byung ba'i sems dang ldan pa dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad rjes su dran pa'i gnas 
[\[17b.3\]]pa las byung ba dang /_'jig rten gsum po thams cad snying po med pa las snying por rtog pa dang /_rang gi lus la ma chags pa'i rgyu [#]dang ldan pa dang /_gnas med pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad kyi rjes su dran pa la gnas pa dang /_snying po med pa'i lus las snying po blang ba'i [#]
[\[17b.4\]]bsam pa dang ldan pas yin no/_/de ltar byang chub sems dpa' lce blangs ma thag tu tshig 'jam zhing dga' bar 'gyur ba byams par spyod pa'i sems kyis [#]rgyal po la 'os pa'i seng ge'i khri bzang po la bzhag nas/_slong ba de la mngon par smra ste/_sems ma khros par byas/_
[\[17b.5\]]sems ma smas pa [#]dang /_sems ma nyams pa dang /_sems bdag nyid chen po'i rigs kyis mtho ba dang /_sems sangs rgyas kyi gdung las byung ba dang /_sems ma rnyogs [#]pa'i rgyud dang mthu stobs chen po bskyed pa dang /_sems lus la ma chags pa dang /_sems kha 
[\[17b.6\]]bzhin la mngon par ma zhen cing /_pus mo'i lha nga [#]btsugs pa'i lus su byas te/_rang gi kha nas lce phyung la slong ba'i lus la gtad de tshig 'jam zhing mnyen la dga' bar 'gyur ba byams pa nye bar spyod pa 'byin cing /_khyod nga'i [#]lce khyer la ji ltar khyod dga' bar 'gyur ba dang /_yid dga' 
[\[17b.7\]]ba dang /_yid bde ba dang /_rab tu dga' zhing rangs pa dang /_mos pa skye bar 'a[#]gyur bar ci 'dod pa bzhin gyis shig ces zer ro/_/de mgo yongs su gtong ba na chos thams cad kyi mchog [#]mgo lta bu'i ye shes la 'jug cing /_sems can thams cad kyi [#]yongs su bskyab pa'i mgos [#]
[\[18a\]]
[\[18a.1\]]byang chub khong du chud par byed pa dang /_'gro ba thams cad kyi mchog dpe med pa'i ye shes mgo lta bu mngon par 'dod pa dang /_kun mkhyen pa'i mgo thob pa[#]/_ye shes kyi rgyal po la mos par byed pa dang /_chos thams cad kyi dbang phyug gi mgo mtha' yas pa 
[\[18a.2\]]yongs su rdzogs par 'dod cing /_slong ba mtha' yas pa dga' bas yongs su khyab pa'i sems kyis yin no/_/de ltar byang chub sems dpa' slong ba dag la lag pa dang rkang pa yongs su gtong ba na dad pa'i lag pa dang ldan pa dang /_rjes su phan 'dogs par spyod pa dang /_
[\[18a.3\]]byang chub sems dpa'i rtsal gyis gtong ba'i lag pa brkyang zhing /_sbyin pa la mngon par dga' bar lag pa yongs su gtong ba dang /_rten chen po la rkang pa 'dor ba dang [#]byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la mos pa dang /_tshor bas ma nyams pa'i sbyin pa'i mthus sems bskyed [#]pa dri 
[\[18a.4\]]ma med par gnas shing /_ye shes yongs su dkris pa med pa dang /_chos kyi lus rgyun mi 'chad pa mi shigs pa mi chod pa'i lus dang ldan pa/_bdud thams cad kyis mi bskyod pa/_sems dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyis [#]nye bar brtan zhing 'phel ba/_byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[18a.5\]]thams cad dang /_spyod pa gcig tu gnas shing gtong ba gcig pus nges par 'byung bas yin no/_/de ltar byang chub sems dpa' rang gi lus dral te slong ba dag la khrag 'byin [#]pa na/_byang chub kyi sems kyis [#]rab tu dga' ba dang /_sems byang chub kyi spyod pa 'dod pa dang /_sems 
[\[18a.6\]]tshor bas yongs su ma zin pa dang /_sems slong ba thams cad 'dod pa dang /_sems yongs su len pa thams cad la mi sdang ba dang /_sems byang chub sems dpa'i gtong ba'i lam thams cad du [#]zhugs pa dang /_sems rang gi lus la mi lta zhing mi ldog la rgyun mi 'chad 
[\[18a.7\]]pas rang gi lus kyi khrag sbyin par byed do[#]/_/ye shes kyi skye mched kyi sems theg pa chen po la zhugs pa/_yid ches pa dang /_yid 'dod pa dang /_yid tshim pa dang /_yid rangs pa dang /_yid mgu ba dang /_yid dga' ba dang /_yid bde ba dang /_yid dang bar gyur te/_
[\[18b\]]
[\[18b.1\]]rab tu dga' zhing mos pa dang /_rangs pa dang /_yid bde ba skyes pas [#]yin no/_/slong ba rnams la rang gi lus las [#]sha yongs su gtong ba na dge bar gtong ste/_slong bar [#]'dod pa'i tshig gis khyed nga'i lus las [#]sha dang rkang ci dgos pa sbyin[#]/_khyer cig ces slong ba de [#]dag la smra 
[\[18b.2\]]zhing /_dga' ba mtshungs pa med pa rnam par spel ba'i gtong ba'i sems dang /_byang chub sems dpa' [#]mkhas pa'i tshogs kyis [#]bsten pa'i dge ba'i chos thams cad kyi grogs su gyur pa dang /_'jig rten gyi dri ma bsal ba'i lhag pa'i bsam pa mchog rab dang /_byang chub 
[\[18b.3\]]sems dpa'i mnyam pa nyid kyis brin [#]pa'i sbyin pa rtsom pa chen po yid kyis [#]'dod par gyur pa [#]dang /_bslong [#]ba thams cad yi mi chad [#]par sbyin pa btang [#]ba'i dngos po dag gis shin tu so sor brtags pa'i las kyi rnam par smin pa'i rkyen dang /_'jig rten gyi khams thams cad mngon du 
[\[18b.4\]]'gyur ba'i [#]sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rgyan bkod pa'i mchod pa dang /_'gro ba thams cad la [#]mngon du phyogs pa'i snying rje chen pos yongs su skyob pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyi byang chub mngon du gyur pa'i stobs bcu rnam par spyod pa dang /_'das pa dang ma 'ongs pa dang da ltar byung ba'i byang chub 
[\[18b.5\]]sems dpa' thams cad mngon du gyur pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba gcig gis rim gror [#]bya ba dang /_mi 'jigs pa thams cad mngon du gyur pa'i khyu mchog dang seng ge'i sgrar [#]sgrogs pa dang /_dus gsum mngon du gyur pa'i dus thams cad mnyam pa nyid du shes pa dang /_'jig rten 
[\[18b.6\]]thams cad mngon du gyur pa phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i bskal par brtson pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i smon lam dang /_yongs su yi ma [#]chad pa mngon du gyur pa'i mi skyo ba'i sems bskyed pas yin no/_/byang chub sems dpa' slong ba dag la rang gi snying gtong ba na/_sems 
[\[18b.7\]]sbyin pa'i rigs la [#]bslabs pa dang /_sems pha rol tu phyin pa la sgrub pa dang /_sems byang chub sems dpa'i sbyin pa thams cad la mi rgod [#]par shin tu gnas pa dang /_sems rten du gyur bas [#]slong ba thams cad la ri mor byed pa dang /_lhag pa'i bsam pa yongs su sbyong ba dang /_
[\[19a\]]
[\[19a.1\]]'gro ba thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba'i phyir smon lam chen po la zhugs pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la gnas pa dang /_thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la 'jug cing smon lam ma btang [#]bas yin no/_/de slong ba dag la rgyu ma dang /_mkhal 
[\[19a.2\]]ma dang /_mchin pa dang /_glo ba sbyin pa ni[#]/_slong ba la rab tu dang ba dang /_dga' ba'i rnam pa'i mig dang /_byang chub sems dpa' las byung ba'i byams pa dang /_mi ngal ba'i yid la byed pa'i gtong ba dang /_snying po med pa'i lus las legs par brtags [#]pa'i snying po 
[\[19a.3\]]len pa'i sems dang /_mthar dur khrod du 'gro bar lus rjes su dran pa'i yid la byed pa dang /_lus gzhan la rag las par yid la bya ba dang /_mi rtag pa nyid kyis zin pa'i lus dang /_gzhan gyi zas su sems pas lus la spyang ki dang /_ce spyang dang /_wa dang /_khyi'i 
[\[19a.4\]]zas su rtog cing /_de ltar chos yid la byed pa la zhugs pa'i byang chub sems dpa'/_slong ba de dag la mig mi 'dzums shing sems 'di ltar bskyed de/_gal te bdag gis slong ba 'di la lus las rgyu ma dang /_mkhal ma dang /_mchin pa dang /_glo ba byin kyang rung gal te ma 
[\[19a.5\]]byin kyang rung ste/_mthar tshe zad na 'di ni [#]mi rtag ste tha ma dur khrod du 'gro'o zhes de ltar yid la byed par [#]tshim par byed [#]pa'i rgyud dang /_de lta bu'i chos shes pa'i bsam pa dang /_dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyi [#]'du shes su gnas pas slong ba la lta ba dang /_snying po med pa'i lus las 
[\[19a.6\]]snying po len 'dod pa [#]yin no/_/chos 'dod pas rang gi sha sen mo dang bcas pa gtong ba na yang /_de ltar dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su sngo bar byed do/_/lus btang ba byas nas/_longs spyod dang bsod nams btang ba ni [#]de nyid las byung ba[#]/_de ltar byang chub sems dpa' [#]sbyin 
[\[19a.7\]]gnas dang len pa sna tshogs phyogs gzhan dang gzhan nas 'ongs pa'i gang zag dang /_slong ba phongs pa dang bkren pa mang po tshad med pa byang chub sems dpa'i sgra thos te 'ongs pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' la [#]re ba'i skabs kyis 'ongs pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i sbyin 
[\[19b\]]
[\[19b.1\]]pa dang sngon gyi smon lam thos pa rnams dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i sbyin pa dang smon lam gyi [#]sems kyis mgron du gnyer ba rnams dang /_thams cad gtong du re ba'i yid can rnams 'ongs pa mthong nas/_slong ba la ri mor [#]bya bas mi ngoms pa'i sems kyis slong ba 'ongs 
[\[19b.2\]]pa rnams la bzod pa gsol ba'i sems dang ldan pas bdag nyid phyogs de dag tu song la khyed 'ongs pa'i [#]nyon mongs par mi [#]'gyur bar ri mor bya ba zhig na zhes des spyi bos gtugs [#]te bzod pa gsol lo/_/slong ba thams cad la bzod pa gsol khrus byas lus ngal sos [#]par 
[\[19b.3\]]byas nas/_yo byad ci 'os pa sbyin par byed de/_'di lta ste/_nor bu'i shing rta 'dzam bu'i gling na bzang ba'i na chung rin po ches yongs su gang ba dang /_'di lta ste/_gser gyi shing rta yul gyi na chung rin po che rnam par dag pas yongs su gang ba dang /_'di lta ste bai DUr+ya'i shing rta glu 
[\[19b.4\]]dbyangs dang sil snyan rjes su mthun pa len pas gang ba dang /_de bzhin du shel gyi shing rta bu mo rin po che bzhin bzang zhing cha byad sdug gus shin du brgyan pa gzugs blta na [#]sdug cing tshul mkhas pas gang ba dag sbyin par byed do zhes gsungs pa dang /_de bzhin du de nyid las/_nor bu'i 
[\[19b.5\]]shing rta glang po che cang shes rin po che thams cad kyi dra bas g.yogs pa dang ldan pa phrugs su mthun pas 'dren pa dag rab tu sbyin par byed/_tsan+dan gyi shing rta rin po che'i 'phang [#]lo dang rtsibs sbyar ba/_rin po che'i seng ge'i khri btsugs pa/_rin po che'i gdugs sna tshogs kyis

[\[19b.7\]]bar 'gro zhing drag thal [#]ma khyer bar 'byor ba nas/_spos kyi ngad yid du 'ong zhing rjes su mthun pa dang /_bu dang bu mor bcas pa'i smra ba nye bar sbyor ba dang ldan pa/_spos kyi phye ma sna tshogs gtor bas gtsang sbrar [#]byas pa dag sbyin par byed do zhes gsungs pa dang /_de bzhin du 
[\[20a\]]
[\[20a.1\]]gzhan yang de nyid las/_bdag nyid sems can thams cad la gtong ngam/_sangs rgyas thams cad la rim gror [#]bya bar 'bul [#]lam/_rgyal srid yongs su gtong ngam/_mkhar ram/_grong khyer ram/_rgyal po'i pho brang rgyan thams cad kyis [#]rnam par spud

[\[20a.3\]]ro'i sbyin pa yang byang chub sems dpas [#]sbyin par byed pa na/_bzang zhing rnam pa sna tshogs pa rgya che ba gtsang ba rnam pa tha dad pa kha ba dang tsha ba dang bska ba dang /_lan tshwa'i ro mchog sna tshogs dang ldan pa shin tu tshim par byed pa'i ro bro ba [#]mang po dang ldan pa dang /_khams 'khrugs 
[\[20a.4\]]pa snyoms par byed pa/_lus dang sems kyi stobs ston [#]pa/_dga' ba dang mos pa dang mchog tu dga' ba dang /_bde ba skyed par byed [#]pa nas/_pha rol gyi gnod par byed pa sel ba/_nad thams cad zhi zhing nad med pa bskyed pa dag sbyin par byed do/_/de bzhin du gos sbyin pa dang /_
[\[20a.5\]]me tog sbyin pa dang /_spos [#]sbyin pa dang /_byug pa sbyin pa dang /_phreng ba sbyin pa dang /_mal cha sbyin pa dang /_gnas sbyin pa dang /_rten sbyin pa dang /_mar me sbyin pa dang /_na ba'i gsos sman sbyin pa dang /_yo byad nas kha zas rnam pa sna tshogs yo lang [#]cher bshams pa dag byang chub sems 
[\[20a.6\]]dpas sbyin par byed do/_/bzo sna tshogs su byas pa'i sder gyi snod sna [#]tshogs pa tshad med pas spras pa rnam pa du ma dang /_dbyig dang gser dang dngul gyi phye ma rnams/_sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das dag [#]la dbul ba na/_sbyin pa'i gnas bsam gyis mi khyab par mos so/_/'di dag 
[\[20a.7\]]byang chub sems dpa' [#]dkon mchog la dbul na/_dge ba'i bshes gnyen shin tu rnyed dka' bas sems bskyed pas dbul la[#]/_/'phags pa'i dge 'dun la 'bul [#]na sangs rgyas kyi chos nye bar brten [#]pa'i phyir dbul lo/_/gang zag gam nyan thos sam rang sangs rgyas dag 
[\[20b\]]
[\[20b.1\]]la 'bul [#]na/_'phags pa'i yon tan la shin tu dang ba'i sems kyis 'bul [#]lo/_/pha ma la sbyin na bla ma la sri zhu dang rim gro bya ba'i sems kyis sbyin no/_/slob dpon dang bla ma dang sbyin pa'i gnas de dang de dag la 'bul [#]na/_bla ma'i tshogs kyis bstan pa bslab pa la 'jug go/_/

[\[20b.3\]]pa/_lo drug [#]lon pa so [#]drug yod pa spu mdog pad+ma 'dra ba/_gdong rnam par dag pa gser gyi [#]rgyan gyis [#]brgyan pa/_lus gser gyi dra bas g.yogs pa/_sna rin po che'i rgyan sna tshogs kyis g.yogs pa/_bkod pas spras pa/_gser gyis [#]g.yogs pa/_blta [#]na sdug pa dpag

[\[20b.5\]]dag sbyin par byed do/_/de dag sbyin zhing gtong ba na gus pas skye bo bla ma dang dge ba'i bshes gnyen dang pha ma dang dbul po dang phongs pa dang slong ba dang len pa'i 'gro ba thams cad la lhug par gtang [#]ba'i sems dang gzung ba med pa'i sems nas/_snying rje chen pos phyogs su khyab pa'i

[\[21a\]]
[\[21a.1\]]gser gyi dra ba'i bla re bres pa/_gser gyi dril bu g.yer kha'i [#]dra ba 'khrol ba'i sgra yid du 'ong ba'i dbyangs 'byung ba/_rin po che'i 'bru bye ba khrag khrig phrag bcus kun nas bskor te rnam par phye bas shing tu [#]'phags shing blta na mdzes pa/_srog chags bye 
[\[21a.2\]]ba khrag khrig grangs med pas kun nas blta [#]ba/_gdugs kheb [#]gcig gis sa chen po thams cad skyong bar dbang bskur ba rgyal srid kyi dbang phyug thams cad la dbang ba'i stan du bya ba/_de la 'dug cing 'khor lo thogs pa med pas bskyang ba dang chis [#]bya ba la rgyal po'i bka' thams cad 
[\[21a.3\]]nyams pa med par nyan pa de lta bu'i sten [#]gyi rnam pa [#]sbyin par byed do/_/de ltar byang chub sems dpa' gdugs rnams sbyin pa na/_rin po che chen po'i bkod pas rab tu brgyan pa/_rin po che'i yu ba can dril bu g.yer kha'i [#]dra bas kun nas khebs pa rin po che'i srad bu dang rna rgyan dang 
[\[21a.4\]]mgul rgyan gyi phreng bas khugs can du gyur pa/_nor bu dang [#]bai DUr+ya'i phreng ba mngon par dpyangs pa/_nye 'khor na dga' ba'i dbyangs dang yid du 'ong ba'i sgra 'byung ba/_gser gyi dra ba rnam par dag pas nang logs khebs par byas pa/_rin po che'i thur ma'i rgyan brgya stong bres 
[\[21a.5\]]pa nor bu rin po che'i mdzod kun du 'dzin pa/_a ka ru dang /_tsan+dan dang /_spos kyi dam pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du mas nye 'khor dri zhim por [#]bsgos shing bdugs pa/_'dzam bu'i chu bo'i gser ltar 'od gsal zhing dag pa/_rgyan bye ba khrag khrig mtha' yas pas brgyan 
[\[21a.6\]]pa'i gdugs de 'dra ba rnams dang /_de bas kyang ches lhag pa'i gdugs bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du ma dang [#]/_/kha na ma tho ba med pa'i sems kyis sbyin par byed cing /_sems can thams cad kyi [#]gtso bo mngon du gyur pa'am/_de bzhin gshegs pa yongs su mya ngan las 'das 
[\[21a.7\]]pa'i mchod rten brgyan pa'i phyir ram/_chos btsal ba'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' dang /_dge ba'i bshes gnyen nam/_byang chub sems dpa' chos smra ba btsun par bya ba dag gam/_pha dang ma'am dge 'dun dkon mchog gam sangs rgyas thams cad kyis [#]bstan pa nas/_len

[\[30b.3\]]bag chags bcas pa'i nyon mongs spong /_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i dam pa'i chos 'dzin pa/_/de la bdud kyis [#]skyon chags rnyed mi 'gyur/_/de la 'gyod dang de bzhin dogs pa med/_/de la bcings med sgrib pa cung zad med/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i dam pa'i chos 'dzin 
[\[30b.4\]]pa/_/zhing stong dag tu nam yang mi skye ste/_/skye ba kun du des ni rgyal ba mthong /_/mthong nas de la dad pa rab tu 'thob/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i dam pa'i chos 'dzin pa/_/chos kyi [#]bdag nyid chen po skye dran 'gyur/_/yang dang yang du rab tu 'byung ba thob[#]/_/spyod pa yongs 
[\[30b.5\]]dag sgrub pa snying por byed/_/de bzhin du sbyar te/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i dam pa'i chos 'dzin pa/_/tshul khrims thob pas 'phags pa'i chos spyod pa/_/mkhas pa [#]mngon shes lnga la [#]myur du reg_/ma sman [#]bsam gtan rnam par thar pa thob[#]/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i dam

[\[31a.1\]]de bzhin gshegs pa'i dam pa'i chos 'dzin pa/_/de la mkhas pa rtag tu dga' bar 'gyur/_/lha dang lha min mi ci'i yid du 'ong /_/de la sangs rgyas bu gcig bzhin du bstod[#]/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i dam pa'i chos 'dzin pa/_/brgya byin tshangs pa

[\[31a.3\]]bas [#]ngoms par yong ma [#]'gyur/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i dam pa'i chos 'dzin pa/_/gang yang [#]chos kyi phyag rgya 'di ston pa'i/_/dge ba'i bshes gnyen de la dkon mi 'gyur/_/chos mdzod zad mi shes pa nyan par byed/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i dam pa'i chos 'dzin pa/_/

[\[33a.1\]]la sogs pa la rnam par rtog pa dang /_de bzhin du mkhan po dang slob dpon dang pha ma dang blon po dang nye du dang snag gi gnyen mtshams yid la byed pa dang /_de bzhin du [#]chom rkun yid la byed pa dang /_de bzhin du gos la sogs pa yid la byed pa 'byung ngo /_/gzhan

[\[33b.1\]]byang chub sems dpa'i dgon pa la gnas pa dang rab tu ldan pa'i bdud kyi lcags kyu ste bdun pa'o/_/de bzhin du sbyar te/_bcom ldan 'das gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' dge ba'i bshes gnyen ltar bcos pa'i sdig pa'i grogs po gang dag de la [#]bsdu ba'i dngos po rnams las 'dun pa bzlog pa dang /_sems can

[\[34b.1\]]'di la byang chub sems dpa' mi dge ba'i bshes gnyen thams cad kyi thams cad du yongs su spong ngo /_/mi mthun pa'i yul na gnas pa dang 'jig rten rgyang pan pa'i gsang tshig bsten cing sgom pa dang /_rnyed pa dang bkur sti dang mchod pa dang rim gro la chags pa thams cad nas

[\[35a.1\]]la yang dag par zhugs pa'i sems can rnams la bsngags pa ma yin pa dang mi snyan pa dang brjod pa ma yin pa dang /_tshigs su bcad pa ma yin pa 'byin pa dang /_sgyu dang g.yos gzhan la nye bar spyod kyi lhag pa'i bsam pas ma yin pa zhes gsungs so/_/

[\[39b.1\]]par byas pa de [#]bzhin du/_snga ma'i blo dang 'dra ba gzhan dag gi mdun du/_don bzang po dang tshig 'bru bzang po rgyas par dran par byed cing rab tu ston la/_de dag ni nan tan tu ma sbyangs pa'i [#]byis pa so so'i skye bo dag ste/_mnyan nas skrag cing dngang ste dngang bar 'gyur zhing /_de dag 
[\[39b.2\]]dngangs [#]nas bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi sems las phyir log ste nyan thos kyi byang chub la smon na 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' las dang po pa'i rtsa ba'i ltung ba dang po ste/_rtsa ba'i ltung ba des rigs kyi bu de dge ba'i rtsa ba sngon skyes [#]pa thams cad thal bar rlog ste pham 
[\[39b.3\]]par gyur cing mtho ris dang thar pa'i bde ba'i gnas las ltung ba dang de'i byang chub kyi [#]sems kyang bslus shing ngan song du 'gro bar 'gyur ro/_/de lta bas na byang chub sems dpa' sems can gzhan dag dang [#]gang zag gzhan rnams kyi bsam pa dang bag la nyal shes par byas la/_bsam pa 
[\[39b.4\]]ji lta ba bzhin du sems can rnams la bags kyis [#]chos bstan par bya ste/_dper na rgya mtsho chen por bags kyis 'jug pa bzhin du bya'o/_/de bzhin du sbyar te/_gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' [#]las dang po pa kha cig la 'di skad du khyod kyis [#]pha rol tu sbyin [#]pa drug la spyod pa spyad

[\[40a.1\]]sems dpa'i las dang po pa'i rtsa ba'i ltung ba gsum pa ste gong du ji skad smos pa'i bar du'o/_/rigs kyi bu gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' las dang po pas kha cig la 'di skad du rigs kyi bu dag khyed nyan thos kyi theg [#]pa'i gtam ma nyan cig_/ma klog cig_/

[\[40b.1\]]des na ngan song du 'gro bar 'gyur ro/_/dper na mi la la zhig rin po che'i gling du 'gro bar 'dod nas gru las rgya mtshor zhugs la/_de rgya mtsho chen po'i nang du bdag nyid kyis gru bshig nas de nyid du 'chi bar 'gyur ba de bzhin du/_byang chub sems dpa' las dang po pa theg pa chen po'i rgya

[\[43b.7\]]ba'i rtsa ba sna tshogs ston par byed pa dang bde 'gro'i lam ston pa yin no/_/de lta bas na nga'i phyir rab tu byung ba tshul khrims dang ldan pa'am tshul khrims 'chal pa yang rung ste/_gang nga'i phyir rab tu byung ba la 'khor los [#]sgyur ba'i rgyal po yang chos dang mthun pas kyang lus la chad

[\[44a.1\]]pas gcod pa dang btson rar 'jug pa dang yan lag dang nying lag gcod pa dang srog gcod du mi gnang na chos ma yin pa lta ci smos/_chos 'dul ba la shi ba lta bur brjod du zin na [#]gang zag de gi wang dang gla rtsi dang mtshungs so [#]zhes gsungs so/_/yang de nyid 
[\[44a.2\]]las/_nga'i [#]phyir rab tu byung ba snod du gyur tam snod du ma gyur kyang rung ba rnams la gang dag 'tshe ba de dag thams cad ni dus gsum du gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas thams cad la shin tu nyes pa dang bcas pa yin no/_/dge ba'i rtsa ba chad pa dang rgyud tshig pa dang mnar med par gzhol 
[\[44a.3\]]ba yin no zhes gsungs pa dang /_yang de nyid las/_'di lta ste/_sangs rgyas thams cad kyis byin gyis brlabs pa'i gos ngur smrig 'di ni thar pa'i rgyal mtshan yin no zhes gsungs pa dang /_yang de nyid las de'i tshe nyan thos khrag khrig brgya stong mang po dang /_byang chub sems

[\[44b.1\]]nyan thos rnams kyi longs spyod pa [#]dang yongs su spyod pa rnams phrogs so zhes 'dzer to/_/kha cig ni bdag cag gis bcom ldan 'das kyi phyir rab tu byung ba rnams khyim par phab ste/_gnas ma lags pa thob par bgyis so zhes 'dzer to/_/kha cig ni bcom ldan

[\[45a.1\]]bram ze rnams la khong khro ba'o/_/mi dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam gyi gnod pa ni dam pa'i chos dran pa nye bar gzhag pa las/_rnam par smin pa drag po gsungs pa lta bur blta ste/_de las cung zad tsam zhig brjod par bya'o/_/srog gcod pa'i rnam par

[\[62b.1\]]tshol/_/yon tan lam la nor [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}#]'di gnas pas/_/'di ni ngan song thams cad sbyong bar 'gyur/_/mtho ris lam yang nye bar ston par gyur[#]/_/thar pa'i lam du 'gro ba 'dzud par 'gyur/_/'di ni 'gro rgyud kun gyi sdug bsngal sel/_/'di ni 'gro rgyud kun gyis [#]

[\[62b.3\]]pa rnam par spang ba gsungs te/_byams pa byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa pa'i gang zag chos bzhi dang ldan na/_phyi ma lnga brgya pa [#]tha ma la dam pa'i [#]chos rnam par 'jig pa'i tshe/_ma snad cing ma smas la bde bar yongs su [#]thar bar 'gyur ro/_/bzhi gang zhe na/_

[\[62b.7\]]su spang ba dang 'du 'dzi la dga' ba'i nyes pa mthong bar bya'o/_/smra ba la dga' ba yongs su spang ba dang /_smra ba la dga' ba'i nyes pa mthong bar bya'o/_/gnyid la dga' ba yongs su spang ba dang /_gnyid la dga' ba'i nyes pa mthong bar bya'o/_/las la dga' ba 
[\[63a\]]
[\[63a.1\]]yongs su spang ba dang las la dga' ba'i nyes pa mthong bar bya'o/_/spros pa la dga' ba yongs su spang ba dang spros pa la dga' ba'i nyes pa mthong bar bya ste/_de bzhin du sbyar ro[#]/_/byams pa 'di la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos rnyed

[\[63a.4\]]pas ngo tsha mi shes pa dang /_khrel med pa bskyed [#]par brtag par bya'o/_/rnyed pa dang bkur sti ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyis ma gnang bar brtag par bya'o/_/rnyed pa dang bkur [#]sti ni nga rgyal dang rgyags pa dang dregs pa bskyed [#]par brtag par bya'o/_/rnyed pa dang bkur sti 
[\[63a.5\]]ni bla ma rnams la khyad du gsod par brtag par bya'o/_/rnyed pa dang bkur sti ni bdud kyi phyogs yin pa dang gcig tu bag med pa'i rtsa ba yin pa dang /_rnyed pa dang bkur sti ni dge ba'i rtsa ba 'phrog pa dang /_rnyed pa dang bkur sti ni glog dang 'khor lo dang lce 'bab pa lta bu dang /_

[\[63b.1\]]bkur sti byed pa [#]la phyis bkur sti mi byed par byed pa dang /_rnyed pa dang bkur sti ni mi mdza' ba dang gnas par byed pa dang mdza' bo spong ba dang /_rnyed pa dang bkur sti ni pha rol 'drid pas smad 'tshong ma 'dra ba dang /_rnyed pa dang bkur sti ni bsam gtan dang tshad med pa yongs su spang ba

[\[63b.4\]]khyim pa dang rab tu byung ba dag yid byung bar mi 'gyur ba yin/_lha dang mi rnams bsam pa yongs su dag par bya ba'i phyir gnas pas rjes su bsrung bar 'os pa yin/_ngan 'gro [#]log par ltung ba thams cad kyis skrag pa med pa yin/_bsdigs pa dang bral bas zil gyis mi non pa

[\[64a.1\]]zhe sdang rab spangs nas/_/rnal 'byor can ni 'du 'dzir mi gnas te/_/de ni de la gzhol zhing de la 'bab/_/nyes pa des na dga' bar byed mi 'gyur/_/dgod dang bzhad gad de bzhin rnam rtog pa/_/'di dag thams cad 'du 'dzi rnams las byung /_/snying po med pa'i

[\[64b.1\]]zhi gnas lhag mthong dag dang rab tu ring /_/smra la dga' ba'i nyes pa 'di dag go/_/rtag tu bla ma dag la gus pa med/_/spags pa'i gtam dag la ni dga' ba skyed/_/snying po med la gnas shing shes rab nyams/_/smra la dga' ba'i nyes pa 'di dag go/_/de ni lha tshogs

[\[65a.1\]]de lta bas na smra la dga' spangs te/_/rtag tu bag yod byos la don soms shig_/ces gsungs so/_/gnyid la dga' ba'i nyes pa'i dbang du byas te/_gang zhig rmugs dang gnyid la dga' 'gyur ba/_/de ni gti mug dra ba chen po 'phel/_/de ni lta

[\[66a.1\]]gshegs pa'i bstan pa la rab tu byung ba ma yin no/_/byams pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i bstan pa ni bsam gtan dang spong bas rab tu phye ba/_ye shes kyis 'dus byas pa ye shes kyis mnyam par gzhag pa/_mngon par brtson pas rab tu phye ba yin gyi khyim

[\[66a.7\]]gsungs so/_/spros pa la dga' ba'i dbang du byas nas/_/de [#]ni mi khom brgyad dang ring mi 'gyur/_/de ni dal ba phun tshogs mchog mi 'gyur/_/de la gnod pa 'di dag rtag 'byung ste/_/spros spyod de yi nyes pa de dag go/_/de bzhin du sbyar te/_/mkhas pas nyes 'di yang 
[\[66b\]]
[\[66b.1\]]dag rig nas ni/_/spros pa thams cad yongs su spang bar bya/_/spros spyod dag ni gnod pa rnyed par sla/_/de lta bas na spros la gnas mi bya/_/gang na spros pa'am rtsod pa yod pa las/_/dpag tshad brgya yi pha rol 'gro ba mchog_/gang na nyon mongs yod pa de dag tu/_/yud

[\[66b.3\]]tshogs kyang yod pa min/_/bran pho dran [#]mo med cing dbang phyug med/_/rab tu byung nas rtsod par ma byed cig_/zhi zhing rab tu zhi bas bsten pa yi/_/ngur smrig [#]gos dag dad pas blangs nas ni/_/zhi zhing rab zhi nye bar zhi bar byos/_/spros pa rnam par spongs la bzod pa skyed[#]/_/
[\[66b.4\]]mi bzad [#]sems ni sbrul gdug 'dra bar srungs[#]/_/spros la [#]spyod pa sems can dmyal ba dang /_/dud 'gro gshin rje'i yul dang ring mi 'gyur/_/de lta bas na bzod dang brtson 'grus skyed[#]/_/de bzhin du sbyar te/_theg pa'i stobs 'di yis ni dag 'gyur zhing /_/las kyi sgrib

[\[84b\]]
[\[84b.1\]]can kun skyob pa'i dpal gyis sngon gyi rtogs pa brjod pa bsnyad pa las kyang /_de dag phan tshun brnyas pa'i mi dge ba [#]'dus pas tshe'i tshad kyang [#]nyams par 'gyur/_kha dog dang stobs dang bde ba las kyang yongs su nyams par 'gyur zhes gsungs so/_/'dir ni brgya lam na yang khengs par mi bya'o

[\[84b.7\]]la mkhas pa'i rang bzhin can su zhig mi rtag cing mi gnas pa'i chos mi brtan pa yid brtan du mi rung ba dag la dga' ba'i sems bskyed pa'am/_khengs par 'gyur ba'am/_nga rgyal dang rgyags pas dregs par 'gyur snyam ste/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rnyed pa dang bkur sti dang grags 
[\[85a\]]
[\[85a.1\]]pa dang sgra dang tshigs su bcad pa rnams la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas so zhes gsungs so/_/gzhan yang dman pa'i yid kyis gdol bu lta bur 'jig rten na spyod pa yin/_rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par bsod snyoms slong ba'i 'du shes nye bar gzhag pas dman zhing

[\[86b.1\]]nga rgyal gyi las bcu po de lta bu de dag yongs su spangs nas/_ye shes kyi las bcu thob par 'gyur ro zhes gsungs so[#]/_/de nyid las sems can thams cad la nga rgyal med pa zhes ye shes kyi las legs par bshad do/_/'phags pa yul 'khor skyong gis zhus pa'i mdo las kyang /_rmongs

[\[87a.1\]]pas bstan pa las kyang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag par [#]skye bar bya ba'i phyir sems can thams cad la ston pa ltar gces par byed ces gsungs so/_/stan 'ding [#]ba dang rkang pa 'khru ba la sogs pa'i las byed pa na yang 'jig rten ma dad par 'gyur ba bsrung ba'i

[\[88b.1\]]sems can rnams ni mgu bar bya/_/rgyal po lta zhig khros na yang /_/sems can mi mgu byas pa yis/_/myong bar 'gyur ba gang yin pa/_/dmyal ba'i gnod pa des byed dam/_/rgyal po lta zhig mgu na yang /_/sems can mgu bar byas pa [#]yi/_/thob par 'gyur ba [#]gang yin pa/_/sangs

[\[88b.3\]]bzod pas thob par byed/_/byams sems ldan la mchod byas [#]gang /_/de ni sems can che ba nyid/_/sangs rgyas dang ba'i bsod nams gang /_/de ni sangs rgyas che ba nyid/_/de lta bas na zla ba sgron ma'i mdo las kyang /_byams pa [#]bsgoms pa'i 'bras bu brjod de/_/mchod pa tshad med rnam [#]mang 
[\[88b.4\]]ji snyed pa/_/zhing ni bye ba khrag khrig gtams snyed du/_/skyes bu mchog la mchod pa de byas pas/_/byams pa'i sems la grangs dang char mi phod/_/ces bya ba'o[#]/_/de lta bas na sbyin pa'i gnas chen po de lta bu dag la khengs pa rtag tu spang bar bya/_khengs pa 'di ni tshul bzhin ma yin pa yid 
[\[88b.5\]]la byed pa las byung ba'i phyir de la mi 'jug par sgrim par bya ste/_dkon mchog sprin las ji skad du/_rigs kyi bu ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' tshul bzhin ma yin pa'i yid la byed pa dang bral ba yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' gcig pu [#]lkog tu song ste/_rab tu dben par gnas 
[\[88b.6\]]shing /_'di ltar bdag ni ma 'dres par gnas pa ste/_bdag ni rab tu dben pa na gnas pa'o/_/bdag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos 'dul ba la zhugs pa'o/_/dge sbyong [#]ngam bram ze gzhan gang yin pa dag ni kun kyang 'dres pas gnas pa/_'du 'dzi mang ba de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos 
[\[88b.7\]]'dul ba las nyams pa'o snyam du sems mi skyed de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' tshul bzhin ma yin pa'i yid la byed pa dang bral ba yin no zhes gsungs pa lta bu'o/_/yang de nyid las 'di la byang chub sems dpa' brtson 'grus rtsom pa ni [#]brtson 'grus de'i ro myong [#]bar mi byed de/_brtson 
[\[89a\]]
[\[89a.1\]]'grus des gzhan la mi smod bdag la mi stod [#]do/_/de 'di snyam du shes rab dang ldan pa'i rang bzhin can su zhig bdag gi las la brtson pas gzhan la smod snyam du sems te/_rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' bsnyems pa med pa'i brtson 'grus can 
[\[89a.2\]]yin no zhes gsungs so/_/bsod nams bsrung ba mdor bsdus pa ni 'di yin te/_byang chub tu yongs su bsngo ba gang yin pa'o/_/ji skad du 'phags pa blo gros mi zad pas bstan pa'i mdo las/_dge ba'i rtsa ba byang chub tu yongs su bsngos pa ni byang chub kyi snying po la 'dug gi bar 
[\[89a.3\]]du bar ma dor 'ga' yang zad par mi 'gyur ro/_/btsun pa sha ra dwa [#]ti'i bu 'di lta ste/_dper na chu'i thigs pa rgya mtsho chen por lhung ba ni bskal pa'i mthar [#]thug gi bar du bar ma dor zad pa med do zhes gsungs so/_/bslab pa kun las btus pa las longs spyod dang bsod nams bsrung ba'i le'u ste

[\[89a.5\]]pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba'i mdo las ji skad du gang yang grong khyer chen po dag gi dur khrod chen po srog chags brgya stong du mas gang bar gyur pa der yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po des bdag gi lus chen po shi zhing dus las 'das par ston to/_/der [#]sems can dud 'gro'i

[\[89b.1\]]dang don du gnyer ba grub pa dang smon lam grub pa yin no zhes gsungs pa lta bu'o/_/yang de nyid las de chos kyi skus rab tu phye bas na mthong bas kyang sems can rnams kyi don byed do/_/thos pa dang reg pas kyang sems can rnams kyi don byed do/_/zhi ba'i blo gros 'di lta ste dper na/_sman

[\[172a.2\]]gshegs/_/sa dang ri dang ngam pa g.yo bar mdzad/_/bcom ldan 'das sku mtshan gyis [#]brgyan pa ste/_/khyod kyi pags pa 'jam zhing gser mdog 'dra/_/'gro ba gzugs 'di lta bas ngoms mi 'gyur/_/khyod ni mtshungs pa ma mchis gzugs dang ldan/_/khyod sngon bskal pa brgyar ni 
[\[172a.3\]]dka' thub spyad/_/khyod gtong dul dang sbyin pa kun la dgyes/_/sems can kun la thugs rje byams thugs ldan/_/mchog tu thugs rje can la phyag 'tshal lo/_/khyod ni sbyin dang tshul khrims rtag tu dgyes/_/khyod ni bzod dang brtson 'grus dges rab brtan/_/khyod ni bsam gtan shes 
[\[172a.4\]]rab 'od gzigs [#]mnga'/_/mtshungs med ye shes mnga' la phyag 'tshal lo/_/khyod ni smra ba ngan pa'i tshogs can 'joms/_/khyod ni seng ge lta bur 'khor du sgrogs/_/khyod ni sman pa'i rgyal po dri gsum sel/_/mchog tu dga' mdzad khyod [#]la phyag 'tshal lo/_/thub pa sku gsung 
[\[172a.5\]]thugs ni rnam par dag_/pad ma chu ltar srid pa gsum mi gos/_/khyod ni tshangs dbyangs ka la ping ka'i skad/_/srid gsum pha rol gshegs [#]la phyag 'tshal lo/_/khyod kyis [#]'gro 'di sgyu ma lta bu dang /_/gar la lta dang rmi lam 'dra ba dang /_/bdag med sems can med cing srog 'gro

[\[181a.2\]]de chos la brtson gyis zhes/_/brjod pas chos rab brjod pa de thob po/_/sangs rgyas sras rnams yongs su bskul ba'i phyir/_/dbyangs dang ldan pa'i 'od zer rab gtong zhing /_/'jig rten gsum na sgra rgyun ji snyed pa/_/kun kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung du thos/_/gsang 
[\[181a.3\]]bstod sgra yis [#]drang srong che bstod de/_/rgyal ba'i gsung sgra don du sil snyan dang /_/sgra chen dril bu sbyin rnams 'gro kun la/_/rab tu bsngags pas dbyangs ldan 'od grub po/_/'chi med sbyin pa'i 'od zer rab gtong [#]zhing /_/sems can gang dag 'od des bskul byas pa/_/
[\[181a.4\]]bag med spyod pa thams cad rab spangs te/_/yon tan kun la brtson pa nyams su len/_/'dus byas rtag tu bde ba med pa ste/_/sdug bsngal 'tshe ba du mas yongs su gang /_/'gog pa rtag tu bde zhing zhi spyod ces/_/brjod pas 'chi med sbyin pa de thob po/_/khyad par ldan 
[\[181a.5\]]pa'i 'od zer rab gtong zhing /_/sems can gang dag 'od des bskul byas pa/_/rgyal ba rnams kyi tshul khrims khyad par dang /_/ting 'dzin khyad par shes rab khyad par thob/_/thub chen rgyal po khrims kyi [#]mchog gyur cing /_/ting 'dzin mchog dang shes rab mchog gyur 
[\[181a.6\]]ces/_/byang chub don du bstod cing bsngags brjod pas/_/des na khyad par ldan pa'i 'od grub po/_/rin chen bkod pa'i 'od zer rab gtong zhing /_/sems can gang dag 'od des bskul byas pa/_/rin chen mi zad gter rnams thob gyur nas/_/drang srong che la rin chen dam pas 
[\[181a.7\]]mchod/_/rgyal dang rgyal ba'i mchod rten rin chen phul/_/skye bo nyon mongs rin chen rnams kyis bsdus/_/rgyal ba rnams la rin chen rab phul bas/_/rin chen bkod pa'i 'od zer grub par gyur[#]/_/spos rab snang ba'i 'od zer rab gtong zhing /_/sems can gang dag 'od des

[\[181b.1\]]bskul byas pas/_/mi la med spos yid 'ong tshor gyur nas/_/sangs rgyas yon tan rnams la nges par 'gyur/_/mi dang lha yi spos dang byug pa yis/_/mi yi gtso la mchod pa rab byas te/_/spos las rgyal ba'i gzugs dang mchod rten dag_/byas pas spos rab snang ba de 
[\[181b.2\]]grub po/_/sna tshogs bkod pa'i 'od zer rab gtong zhing /_/rgyal mtshan ba dan gdugs mchog sna tshogs dang /_/sil snyan bsgrags shing spos kyi bdug pa yis/_/pho brang dam pa mdzes byas me tog bkram/_/sil snyan dag gis rgyal ba rnams bsu zhing /_/me tog 
[\[181b.3\]]bdug pa bdug spos phye ma dang /_/rgyal mtshan ba dan gdugs mchog rnams kyis mchod/_/des na sna tshogs bkod pa grub par gyur/_/shin tu dang byed 'od zer rab gtong zhing /_/sa rnams lag mthil bzhin du gnas par byed/_/mchod rten drang srong gnas rnams 
[\[181b.4\]]sbyang byas pas/_/des na shin tu dang byed 'od grub po/_/sprin dang ldan pa'i 'od zer rab gtong zhing /_/spos kyi char 'bebs sprin rnams kun du gnas/_/mchod rten 'khor sar spos kyi [#]chus btang pas/_/sprin dang ldan pa'i 'od de rab tu 'thob/_/lhab lhub bkod pa'i 
[\[181b.5\]]'od zer rab gtong zhing /_/gcer bu gos gyon rgyan dang ldan par 'gyur/_/gos dang bgo ba rgyan phreng sna tshogs dag_/sbyin pa byas pas lhab lhub bkod pa grub/_/ro mchog ldan pa'i 'od zer rab gtong zhing /_/bkres pa kha zas ro mchog thob par 'gyur/_/zas dang skom 
[\[181b.6\]]dang ro mchog sna tshogs rnams/_/byin pas ro mchog ldan pa'i 'od grub po/_/nor rnams ston pa'i 'od zer rab gtong zhing /_/dbul po dag gis rin chen gter rnams 'thob/_/dkon mchog gsum la gter gzhi mi zad pa/_/dbul ba byas pas nor ston 'od thob po/_/

[\[182b.1\]]'jam rnams dang /_/phreng ba bla re phul ba'i 'od de yis/_/chos rnam sbyong ba'i 'od zer rab gtong zhing /_/'jig rten phan mdzad rgyal ba thams cad kyi/_/spu rnams kun nas chos rnams bsam mi khyab/_/byung ba thos nas mos pa thams cad tshim/_/rkyen las byung bas ma

[\[185b.1\]]sdang gti mug ldan [#]de yang /_/skad rnams kun gyi [#]sgra yi tshul mkhas na/_/gzungs [#]dang chos kyi stobs la dbang thob rnams/_/lhar bcas 'jig rten ci phyir mgu mi byed/_/tam bu ra [#]yi chung ma dbyangs ldan ma/_/tshangs pa'i bu mo yid la byung ba de/_/bden dang dam bcas mi 
[\[185b.2\]]tshe [#]dga' rnams kyi/_/sems dang yid dang kha yi sgor 'jug ste/_/dbyangs ldan chags bcas zhe sdang can de yang /_/brtul zhugs grub mthun dbyangs kyi yan lag gis/_/yid 'ong skad kyis 'jig rten mgu byed na/_/ye shes yon tan phyug [#]rnams cis mi byed/_/rig sngags 
[\[185b.3\]]tshul mkhas sgyu ma'i mkhan po ni/_/sna tshogs gzugs mang mtha' yas ston pa [#]byed/_/nyin mtshan yud dang zla ba lo brgya dang /_/khyim bzang rgyas pa shin tu mdzes pa ston/_/'dod chags sdang ldan sgyu ma'i mkhan po yang /_/sgyu ma'i 'phrul gyis 'jig rten mgu byed na/_/
[\[185b.4\]]bsam gtan mngon shes rnam thar rab bslabs pa/_/spyod tshul mkhas rnams ci phyir mgu mi byed/_/lha dang lha min dbang po 'thab pa'i tshe/_/rgyal ldan lha yis lha min pham byas na/_/de dag 'jigs pas gdugs bcas bzhon bcas shing /_/theg bcas dmag bcas go 
[\[185b.5\]]gyon bzhin du 'byer[#]/_/'dod chags sdang ldan lha dbang de dag kyang /_/lus kyi rnam 'phrul bsam yas ston byed na/_/dbang phyug rdzu 'phrul dbang gnas dpa' ba rnams/_/lus kyi rnam 'phrul ci phyir ston mi byed/_/rdo rje 'chang ba'i glang po sa srung [#]bu/_/dbang po'i 'khor gyur rdzu

[\[186a.1\]]mchog gi rnam 'phrul de ltar snang /_/glang po chags sdang gti mug ldan de yang /_/'di 'dra'i rdzu 'phrul rab tu ston byed na/_/shes rab thabs kyis stobs la sbyangs pa rnams/_/ting 'dzin brgya yi rnam 'phrul cis mi byed/_/sgra gcan ji ltar 'dod bzhin lus sprul

[\[194b.1\]]kun gyi slob par [#]bya/_/nyon mongs dgra ni mang po dang /_/gcig pu bdag ni g.yul 'gyed tshe/_/de na gcig dang 'thab pa na/_/gzhan ni bdag gi bde ba 'joms/_/sdang ngam mi sdang gyur kyang rung /_/de na la la rgyab nas sam[#]/_/gzhan nas 'jigs pa sgrogs 
[\[194b.2\]]byed pa/_/de ni bdag la srog phangs sbyin/_/sdug bsngal kun gyi sman pa mchog_/bde ba'i dga' ston rab tshogs zhing /_/rnam pa kun du nyer 'tsho ba/_/'jam dbyangs khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/de ltar bde bar gshegs kyi sras/_/rnam kun rmad du byung rnams 
[\[194b.3\]]kyi/_/spyod pa nye bar bkod pa yi/_/dge ba bdag gis gang bsags pa[#]/_/bde gshegs go 'phang mtha' yas ni/_/nam mkha'i mtshams par dbang mdzad pa/_/de yi bde ba mtha' yas rnams/_/lus can dag gis thob par shog_/bsod nams spel ba 
[\[194b.4\]]rdzogs so//_//bslab pa kun las btus pa zhes bya ba byang chub sems dpa'i 'dul ba mdo sde du ma las btus pa/_slob dpon zhi ba'i lhas mdzad pa rdzogs so//_//rgya gar gyi mkhan po dzi na mi tra dang /_dA na shI la dang /_zhu chen gyi lo tsA [#]ba ban de ye shes sdes
